### `app/admin/products/actions.ts`
```typescript
"use server";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import fs from "fs/promises";
import path from "path";
import { requirePermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

const MIME_TO_EXT: Record<string, string> = {
  "image/jpeg": "jpg",
  "image/png": "png",
  "image/webp": "webp",
};
const MAX_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

type VariantInput = {
  id?: string;
  name: string;
  sku?: string | null;
  price?: number | string | null;
  stock?: number | string;
};

type ActionResult = {
  success: boolean;
  message?: string;
};

async function saveImage(file: File) {
  const ext = MIME_TO_EXT[file.type];
  if (!ext) throw new Error("فرمت تصویر مجاز نیست (فقط JPG، PNG و WebP).");
  if (file.size > MAX_SIZE) throw new Error("حجم تصویر بیش از ۵ مگابایت است.");

  const uploadDir = path.join(process.cwd(), "public", "uploads", "products");
  await fs.mkdir(uploadDir, { recursive: true });

  const bytes = await file.arrayBuffer();
  const buffer = Buffer.from(bytes);

  const fileName = `${Date.now()}-${crypto.randomUUID()}.${ext}`;
  const filePath = path.join(uploadDir, fileName);

  await fs.writeFile(filePath, buffer);
  return `/uploads/products/${fileName}`;
}

async function deleteImageFile(url: string) {
  try {
    const filePath = path.join(process.cwd(), "public", url);
    await fs.unlink(filePath);
  } catch {
    console.error("Error deleting file:", url);
  }
}

function resolvePriceAndStock(formData: FormData, parsedVariants: VariantInput[]) {
  const basePriceRaw = formData.get("price");
  const basePrice =
    basePriceRaw !== null && String(basePriceRaw).trim() !== ""
      ? Number(basePriceRaw)
      : 0;

  let finalPrice = basePrice;
  let finalStock = Number(formData.get("stock"));

  if (parsedVariants.length > 0) {
    finalStock = parsedVariants.reduce(
      (sum, v) => sum + (Number(v.stock) || 0),
      0
    );

    const hasVariantWithoutCustomPrice = parsedVariants.some(
      (v) => !v.price || Number(v.price) <= 0
    );

    if (hasVariantWithoutCustomPrice) {
      // اگر حداقل یک تنوع قیمت مجزا نداشته باشد، باید قیمت پایه معتبر باشد تا قیمت آن تنوع حفظ شود
      if (Number.isNaN(basePrice) || basePrice <= 0) {
        throw new Error(
          "برای تنوع‌هایی که قیمت مجزا ندارند، وارد کردن قیمت پایه محصول الزامی است."
        );
      }
      finalPrice = basePrice;
    } else {
      const variantPrices = parsedVariants.map((v) => Number(v.price));
      finalPrice = Math.min(...variantPrices);
    }
  }

  if (Number.isNaN(finalPrice) || finalPrice <= 0) {
    throw new Error("قیمت محصول نامعتبر است. لطفاً قیمت معتبر وارد کنید.");
  }

  finalStock =
    Number.isNaN(finalStock) || finalStock < 0 ? 0 : Math.floor(finalStock);

  return { finalPrice, finalStock };
}

function formatPrismaError(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError && error.code === "P2002") {
    return "این اسلاگ یا کد محصول (SKU) قبلاً ثبت شده است.";
  }
  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : "خطایی در ثبت اطلاعات رخ داد.";
}

/* =========================
   🟢 CREATE PRODUCT
========================= */
export async function createProduct(formData: FormData): Promise<ActionResult | void> {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_PRODUCTS");

  try {
    const title = String(formData.get("title") || "").trim();
    const slug = String(formData.get("slug") || "").trim();
    const description = String(formData.get("description") || "").trim();

    const skuRaw = formData.get("sku") as string;
    const sku = skuRaw && skuRaw.trim() !== "" ? skuRaw.trim() : null;

    const brandIdRaw = formData.get("brandId") as string;
    const brandId = brandIdRaw && brandIdRaw.trim() !== "" ? brandIdRaw : null;

    const categoryId = formData.get("categoryId") as string;
    if (!categoryId) {
      return { success: false, message: "انتخاب دسته‌بندی الزامی است." };
    }

    const offerPriceValue = formData.get("offerPrice");
    const offerPrice =
      offerPriceValue && offerPriceValue !== "" ? Number(offerPriceValue) : null;

    const offerStartsAtValue = formData.get("offerStartsAt");
    const offerEndsAtValue = formData.get("offerEndsAt");

    const offerStartsAt =
      offerStartsAtValue && offerStartsAtValue !== ""
        ? new Date(offerStartsAtValue as string)
        : null;

    const offerEndsAt =
      offerEndsAtValue && offerEndsAtValue !== ""
        ? new Date(offerEndsAtValue as string)
        : null;

    const isActive = formData.get("isActive") === "on";

    const variantsRaw = formData.get("variants") as string;
    const parsedVariants: VariantInput[] = variantsRaw ? JSON.parse(variantsRaw) : [];
    const { finalPrice, finalStock } = resolvePriceAndStock(formData, parsedVariants);

    const product = await prisma.product.create({
      data: {
        title,
        slug,
        sku,
        brandId,
        description,
        price: finalPrice,
        stock: finalStock,
        categoryId,
        isActive,
        offerPrice,
        offerStartsAt,
        offerEndsAt,
        variants: {
          create: parsedVariants.map((v) => ({
            name: v.name,
            sku: v.sku && String(v.sku).trim() !== "" ? String(v.sku).trim() : null,
            price: v.price && Number(v.price) > 0 ? Number(v.price) : null,
            stock: Number(v.stock) || 0,
          })),
        },
      },
    });

    await processOrderedImages(formData, product.id);
  } catch (error) {
    return { success: false, message: formatPrismaError(error) };
  }

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/products");
  redirect("/admin/products");
}

/* =========================
   🟡 UPDATE PRODUCT
========================= */
export async function updateProduct(
  id: string,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<ActionResult | void> {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_PRODUCTS");

  let slug = "";

  try {
    const title = String(formData.get("title") || "").trim();
    slug = String(formData.get("slug") || "").trim();
    const description = String(formData.get("description") || "").trim();

    const skuRaw = formData.get("sku") as string;
    const sku = skuRaw && skuRaw.trim() !== "" ? skuRaw.trim() : null;

    const brandIdRaw = formData.get("brandId") as string;
    const brandId = brandIdRaw && brandIdRaw.trim() !== "" ? brandIdRaw : null;

    const categoryId = formData.get("categoryId") as string;
    if (!categoryId) {
      return { success: false, message: "انتخاب دسته‌بندی الزامی است." };
    }

    const offerPriceValue = formData.get("offerPrice");
    const offerPrice =
      offerPriceValue && offerPriceValue !== "" ? Number(offerPriceValue) : null;

    const offerStartsAtValue = formData.get("offerStartsAt");
    const offerEndsAtValue = formData.get("offerEndsAt");

    const offerStartsAt =
      offerStartsAtValue && offerStartsAtValue !== ""
        ? new Date(offerStartsAtValue as string)
        : null;

    const offerEndsAt =
      offerEndsAtValue && offerEndsAtValue !== ""
        ? new Date(offerEndsAtValue as string)
        : null;

    const isActive = formData.get("isActive") === "on";

    const variantsRaw = formData.get("variants") as string;
    const parsedVariants: VariantInput[] = variantsRaw ? JSON.parse(variantsRaw) : [];
    const { finalPrice, finalStock } = resolvePriceAndStock(formData, parsedVariants);

    await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.product.update({
        where: { id },
        data: {
          title,
          slug,
          sku,
          brandId,
          categoryId,
          description,
          price: finalPrice,
          stock: finalStock,
          isActive,
          offerPrice,
          offerStartsAt,
          offerEndsAt,
        },
      });

      const existingVariants = await tx.productVariant.findMany({
        where: { productId: id },
        select: { id: true },
      });
      const existingIds = new Set(existingVariants.map((v) => v.id));

      const toUpdate = parsedVariants.filter(
        (v): v is VariantInput & { id: string } =>
          Boolean(v.id && existingIds.has(v.id))
      );
      const toCreate = parsedVariants.filter(
        (v) => !v.id || !existingIds.has(v.id)
      );
      const keptIds = new Set(toUpdate.map((v) => v.id));
      const idsToDelete = existingVariants
        .filter((v) => !keptIds.has(v.id))
        .map((v) => v.id);

      if (idsToDelete.length > 0) {
        await tx.productVariant.deleteMany({
          where: { id: { in: idsToDelete } },
        });
      }

      for (const v of toUpdate) {
        await tx.productVariant.update({
          where: { id: v.id },
          data: {
            name: v.name,
            sku: v.sku && String(v.sku).trim() !== "" ? String(v.sku).trim() : null,
            price: v.price && Number(v.price) > 0 ? Number(v.price) : null,
            stock: Number(v.stock) || 0,
          },
        });
      }

      if (toCreate.length > 0) {
        await tx.productVariant.createMany({
          data: toCreate.map((v) => ({
            productId: id,
            name: v.name,
            sku: v.sku && String(v.sku).trim() !== "" ? String(v.sku).trim() : null,
            price: v.price && Number(v.price) > 0 ? Number(v.price) : null,
            stock: Number(v.stock) || 0,
          })),
        });
      }
    });

    await processOrderedImages(formData, id);
  } catch (error) {
    return { success: false, message: formatPrismaError(error) };
  }

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/products");
  revalidatePath(`/products/${slug}`);
  redirect("/admin/products");
}

/* =========================
   🔴 DELETE PRODUCT
========================= */
export async function deleteProduct(id: string) {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_PRODUCTS");

  try {
    const images = await prisma.productImage.findMany({
      where: { productId: id },
      select: { url: true },
    });

    await prisma.product.delete({ where: { id } });
    await Promise.all(images.map((img) => deleteImageFile(img.url)));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError && error.code === "P2003") {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "این محصول در سفارشات کاربران ثبت شده و قابل حذف نیست. لطفاً آن را غیرفعال کنید.",
      };
    }
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "خطایی غیرمنتظره در حذف محصول رخ داد.",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/products");
  redirect("/admin/products");
}

/* =========================
   📁 SORTED IMAGES HELPER
========================= */
async function processOrderedImages(formData: FormData, productId: string) {
  const imageOrderRaw = formData.get("imageOrder") as string;
  if (!imageOrderRaw) return;

  const imageOrder: string[] = JSON.parse(imageOrderRaw);
  const existingImageIdsToKeep = imageOrder.filter(
    (id) => !formData.has(`file_${id}`)
  );

  const imagesToDelete = await prisma.productImage.findMany({
    where: {
      productId,
      id: { notIn: existingImageIdsToKeep },
    },
    select: { url: true },
  });

  await prisma.productImage.deleteMany({
    where: {
      productId,
      id: { notIn: existingImageIdsToKeep },
    },
  });

  await Promise.all(imagesToDelete.map((img) => deleteImageFile(img.url)));

  for (let index = 0; index < imageOrder.length; index++) {
    const id = imageOrder[index];
    const file = formData.get(`file_${id}`) as File | null;

    if (file && file.size > 0) {
      const url = await saveImage(file);
      await prisma.productImage.create({
        data: { url, order: index, productId },
      });
    } else {
      await prisma.productImage.updateMany({
        where: { productId, id },
        data: { order: index },
      });
    }
  }
}

/* =========================
   👁️ TOGGLE PRODUCT STATUS
========================= */
export async function toggleProductStatus(id: string, newStatus: boolean) {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_PRODUCTS");

  try {
    await prisma.product.update({
      where: { id },
      data: { isActive: newStatus },
    });

    revalidatePath("/admin/products");
    revalidatePath("/products");
    revalidatePath("/");

    return {
      success: true,
      message: newStatus
        ? "محصول در فروشگاه فعال شد"
        : "محصول غیرفعال شد و دیگر به کاربران نمایش داده نمی‌شود",
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "خطایی در تغییر وضعیت محصول رخ داد.",
    };
  }
}
```

### `app/admin/products/delete-button.tsx`
```tsx
"use client";

import { useTransition } from "react";
import { deleteProduct } from "./actions";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Trash2, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

export function DeleteProductButton({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  const handleDelete = () => {
    if (!window.confirm("آیا از حذف این محصول مطمئن هستید؟")) return;

    startTransition(async () => {
      const result = await deleteProduct(id);

      if (result && !result.success) {
        toast.error(result.message, {
          style: {
            background: "#fee2e2",
            color: "#991b1b",
            border: "1px solid #f87171",
          },
        });
      }
    });
  };

  return (
    <Button
      onClick={handleDelete}
      disabled={isPending}
      variant="destructive"
      className="flex-shrink-0 rounded-xl h-9 w-9 flex items-center justify-center p-0 shadow-sm"
      title="حذف محصول"
    >
      {isPending ? (
        <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
      ) : (
        <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
      )}
    </Button>
  );
}
```

### `app/admin/products/page.tsx`
```tsx
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requirePermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
import Link from "next/link";
import Image from "next/image";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { ProductFilters } from "@/components/product/product-filters";
import { ProductPagination } from "@/components/product/product-pagination";

import { DeleteProductButton } from "./delete-button";
import { ToggleStatusButton } from "./toggle-status-button";

import { Plus, Edit, Layers, Package, ShoppingBag } from "lucide-react";

type Props = {
  searchParams: Promise<{
    page?: string;
    q?: string;
    category?: string;
    sort?: string;
  }>;
};

const PAGE_SIZE = 12;

export default async function AdminProductsPage({ searchParams }: Props) {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_PRODUCTS");

  const { page, q, category, sort } = await searchParams;
  const rawPage = Number(page);
  const currentPage = Number.isInteger(rawPage) && rawPage > 0 ? rawPage : 1;

  const where = {
    ...(q
      ? {
          OR: [
            { title: { contains: q, mode: "insensitive" as const } },
            { sku: { contains: q, mode: "insensitive" as const } },
          ],
        }
      : {}),
    ...(category
      ? {
          category: {
            OR: [{ slug: category }, { parent: { slug: category } }],
          },
        }
      : {}),
  };

  const totalProducts = await prisma.product.count({ where });
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalProducts / PAGE_SIZE);

  const orderBy =
    sort === "price_asc"
      ? { price: "asc" as const }
      : sort === "price_desc"
        ? { price: "desc" as const }
        : { createdAt: "desc" as const };

  const products = await prisma.product.findMany({
    where,
    include: {
      category: true,
      images: { orderBy: { order: "asc" } },
    },
    orderBy,
    skip: (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE,
    take: PAGE_SIZE,
  });

  const categories = await prisma.category.findMany({
    orderBy: { name: "asc" },
  });

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto py-8 px-4 space-y-8 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-zinc-100">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
          <div className="p-3 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-100 rounded-2xl text-zinc-700 shadow-sm">
            <ShoppingBag className="h-6 w-6" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-zinc-900 tracking-tight">محصولات</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">
              لیست کالاها و مدیریت دقیق موجودی کل فروشگاه ({totalProducts.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} کالا)
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <Button asChild size="sm" className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2">
          <Link href="/admin/products/new">
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>محصول جدید</span>
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>

      <ProductFilters
        categories={categories}
        searchParams={{ q, category, sort }}
        actionUrl="/admin/products"
      />

      {products.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="border-dashed border-zinc-200 p-12 text-center bg-zinc-50/20">
          <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-4">
            <div className="p-4 bg-zinc-50 rounded-full text-zinc-400 border border-zinc-100">
              <Package className="h-10 w-10" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-800">هیچ محصولی یافت نشد</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">جستجوی شما نتیجه‌ای در بر نداشت یا محصولی ثبت نشده است.</p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
          {products.map((product) => {
            const imageUrl = product.images?.[0]?.url || "/placeholder.png";
            const isOutOfStock = product.stock <= 0;

            return (
              <Card
                key={product.id}
                className="border-zinc-100 shadow-sm rounded-2xl overflow-hidden hover:shadow-md transition duration-200 flex flex-col justify-between bg-white"
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="relative aspect-square bg-zinc-50 border-b border-zinc-50 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                    <Image
                      src={imageUrl}
                      alt={product.title}
                      fill
                      sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 250px"
                      className={`object-contain p-5 transition-all duration-300 ${
                        !product.isActive ? "opacity-40 grayscale" : ""
                      }`}
                    />

                    <div className="absolute top-3.5 right-3.5 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                      <span
                        className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold shadow-sm ${
                          isOutOfStock
                            ? "bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-100"
                            : "bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-100"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {isOutOfStock ? "ناموجود" : `${product.stock} عدد`}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {!product.isActive && (
                      <div className="absolute top-3.5 left-3.5">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[9px] font-bold bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-50 border border-zinc-900 shadow-sm">
                          غیرفعال
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-4 space-y-2.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-zinc-400 text-[10px] font-semibold">
                      <Layers className="h-3 w-3 text-zinc-400" />
                      <span>{product.category.name}</span>
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-800 line-clamp-1" title={product.title}>
                      {product.title}
                    </h3>
                    <div className="flex flex-col text-left">
                      <span className="font-extrabold text-base text-zinc-900 tracking-tight">
                        {Number(product.price).toLocaleString("fa-IR")}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-zinc-400">تومان</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-4 pt-0 border-t border-zinc-50/60 mt-auto flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <Button
                    asChild
                    variant="outline"
                    className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold py-2 h-9 rounded-xl border-zinc-200 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-50 transition"
                  >
                    <Link href={`/admin/products/${product.id}/edit`}>
                      <Edit className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-500" />
                      <span>ویرایش</span>
                    </Link>
                  </Button>

                  <ToggleStatusButton id={product.id} isActive={product.isActive} />
                  <DeleteProductButton id={product.id} />
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      <ProductPagination
        currentPage={currentPage}
        totalPages={totalPages}
        baseUrl="/admin/products"
        searchParams={{ q, category, sort }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `app/admin/products/toggle-status-button.tsx`
```tsx
"use client";

import { useTransition } from "react";
import { toggleProductStatus } from "./actions"; // مسیر اکشن را در صورت نیاز اصلاح کنید
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Eye, EyeOff, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

export function ToggleStatusButton({ id, isActive }: { id: string; isActive: boolean }) {
  const [isPending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  const handleToggle = () => {
    startTransition(async () => {
      // وضعیت جدید برعکس وضعیت فعلی خواهد بود
      const result = await toggleProductStatus(id, !isActive);

      if (result) {
        if (result.success) {
          toast.success(result.message, {
            style: {
              background: "#dcfce7", // bg-green-100
              color: "#166534",      // text-green-800
              border: "1px solid #bbf7d0", // border-green-200
            }
          });
        } else {
          toast.error(result.message);
        }
      }
    });
  };

  return (
    <Button
      onClick={handleToggle}
      disabled={isPending}
      variant="outline"
      title={isActive ? "غیرفعال کردن محصول" : "فعال کردن محصول"}
      className={`rounded-xl h-9 w-9 flex flex-shrink-0 items-center justify-center p-0 shadow-sm transition-all ${isActive ? "hover:bg-amber-50" : "hover:bg-green-50"
        }`}
    >
      {isPending ? (
        <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-zinc-400" />
      ) : isActive ? (
        <Eye className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500 hover:text-amber-600" />
      ) : (
        <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-300 hover:text-green-600" />
      )}
    </Button>
  );
}
```

### `app/admin/settings/actions.ts`
```typescript
"use server";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requirePermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

export async function getOtpSetting(): Promise<boolean> {
  const setting = await prisma.systemSetting.findUnique({
    where: { key: "OTP_ENABLED" },
  });
  return setting ? setting.value === "true" : true;
}

export async function updateOtpSetting(enabled: boolean) {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_SETTINGS");

  await prisma.systemSetting.upsert({
    where: { key: "OTP_ENABLED" },
    update: { value: String(enabled) },
    create: { key: "OTP_ENABLED", value: String(enabled) },
  });

  revalidatePath("/admin/settings");
}
```

### `app/admin/shipping/actions.ts`
```typescript
"use server";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requirePermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

export async function createShippingMethod(
  name: string,
  price: number,
  estimatedTime: string,
  isActive: boolean
) {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_SHIPPING");

  await prisma.shippingMethod.create({
    data: { name: name.trim(), price, estimatedTime: estimatedTime.trim(), isActive },
  });

  revalidatePath("/admin/shipping");
  return { success: true, message: "روش ارسال جدید با موفقیت ثبت شد." };
}

export async function updateShippingMethod(
  id: string,
  name: string,
  price: number,
  estimatedTime: string,
  isActive: boolean
) {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_SHIPPING");

  await prisma.shippingMethod.update({
    where: { id },
    data: { name: name.trim(), price, estimatedTime: estimatedTime.trim(), isActive },
  });

  revalidatePath("/admin/shipping");
  return { success: true, message: "تغییرات با موفقیت ذخیره شد." };
}

export async function toggleShippingMethod(id: string, currentStatus: boolean) {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_SHIPPING");

  await prisma.shippingMethod.update({
    where: { id },
    data: { isActive: !currentStatus },
  });

  revalidatePath("/admin/shipping");
  return {
    success: true,
    message: !currentStatus ? "روش ارسال فعال شد." : "روش ارسال غیرفعال شد.",
  };
}

export async function deleteShippingMethod(id: string) {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_SHIPPING");

  try {
    await prisma.shippingMethod.delete({ where: { id } });
  } catch {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "این روش ارسال در سفارشات قبلی ثبت شده و قابل حذف نیست.",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/admin/shipping");
  return { success: true, message: "روش ارسال با موفقیت حذف شد." };
}
```

```tsx
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export default async function AdminShippingPage() {

  const methods = await prisma.shippingMethod.findMany({

      </div>
```

### `app/admin/users/[id]/edit/page.tsx`
```tsx
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requirePermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
import UserForm from "@/components/admin/user-form";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { ArrowRight, UserCog, Users } from "lucide-react";

type Props = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

export default async function EditUserPage({ params }: Props) {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_USERS");
  const { id } = await params;

  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id } });

  if (!user) {
    notFound();
  }

  const safeUser = {
    id: user.id,
    name: user.name,
    username: user.username,
    phone: user.phone,
    role: user.role,
  };

  return (
    <div className="container max-w-4xl mx-auto py-8 px-4 space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-zinc-100">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
          <div className="p-3 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-100 rounded-2xl text-zinc-700 shadow-sm">
            <UserCog className="h-6 w-6" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-zinc-900 tracking-tight">ویرایش کاربر</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">مشخصات، نام کاربری، موبایل و نقش کاربری را ویرایش کنید.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" asChild className="w-fit rounded-xl border-zinc-200 text-zinc-700">
          <Link href="/admin/users" className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>بازگشت به کاربران</span>
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>

      <Card className="border-zinc-100 shadow-sm rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-white">
        <CardHeader className="border-b border-zinc-100/60 bg-zinc-50/10 pb-4">

          </CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="p-6">
          <UserForm user={safeUser} />
        </CardContent>

  );
```

### `app/admin/users/actions.ts`
```typescript
"use server";

import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requirePermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import bcrypt from "bcrypt";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { Prisma, Role } from "@prisma/client";

type ActionResult = {
  success: boolean;
  message?: string;
};

export async function createUser(formData: FormData): Promise<ActionResult | void> {
  await requirePermission("MANAGE_USERS");

  const name = String(formData.get("name") || "").trim();
  const username = String(formData.get("username") || "").trim();
  const phone = String(formData.get("phone") || "").trim();
  const password = String(formData.get("password") || "");
  const role = formData.get("role") as Role;

  if (!Object.values(Role).includes(role)) {
    return { success: false, message: "نقش کاربری نامعتبر است." };
  }

  const existingUser = await prisma.user.findFirst({
    where: { OR: [{ username }, { phone }] },
  });

  if (existingUser) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "کاربری با این نام کاربری یا شماره موبایل از قبل وجود دارد.",
    };
  }

  const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10);

  await prisma.user.create({
    data: { name, username, phone, password: hashedPassword, role },
  });

  redirect("/admin/users");
}

export async function updateUser(
  id: string,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<ActionResult | void> {
  const admin = await requirePermission("MANAGE_USERS");

  const name = String(formData.get("name") || "").trim();
  const username = String(formData.get("username") || "").trim();
  const phone = String(formData.get("phone") || "").trim();
  const password = String(formData.get("password") || "");
  const role = formData.get("role") as Role;

  if (!Object.values(Role).includes(role)) {
    return { success: false, message: "نقش کاربری نامعتبر است." };
  }

  if (admin.id === id && role !== "ADMIN") {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "شما مجاز به عزل نقش مدیریت خودتان نیستید.",
    };
  }

  const existingUser = await prisma.user.findFirst({
    where: {
      OR: [{ username }, { phone }],
      NOT: { id },
    },
  });

  if (existingUser) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "این نام کاربری یا شماره موبایل توسط کاربر دیگری استفاده شده است.",
    };
  }

  const updateData: Prisma.UserUpdateInput = { name, username, phone, role };

  if (password.trim() !== "") {
    updateData.password = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10);
  }

  await prisma.user.update({ where: { id }, data: updateData });

  redirect("/admin/users");
}

export async function toggleUserRole(id: string): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const admin = await requirePermission("MANAGE_USERS");

  if (admin.id === id) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "شما نمی‌توانید نقش مدیریت خودتان را تغییر دهید.",
    };
  }

  const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id } });
  if (!user) {
    return { success: false, message: "کاربر یافت نشد." };
  }

  const newRole = user.role === "ADMIN" ? "USER" : "ADMIN";

  await prisma.user.update({
    where: { id },
    data: { role: newRole },
  });

  revalidatePath("/admin/users");
  return {
    success: true,
    message:
      newRole === "ADMIN"
        ? "کاربر به مدیر سایت ارتقا یافت."
        : "دسترسی مدیریت از کاربر گرفته شد.",
  };
}

export async function deleteUser(id: string): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const admin = await requirePermission("MANAGE_USERS");

  if (admin.id === id) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "شما نمی‌توانید حساب کاربری خودتان را حذف کنید.",
    };
  }

  try {
    await prisma.user.delete({ where: { id } });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return {
      success: false,
      message:
        "این کاربر دارای تاریخچه خرید فعال در سیستم است و امکان حذف آن وجود ندارد.",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/admin/users");
  return { success: true, message: "کاربر با موفقیت حذف شد." };
}
```

### `app/admin/users/page.tsx`
```tsx
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { requirePermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
import { UserRowActions } from "@/components/admin/user-row-actions";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Role } from "@prisma/client";

import {
  Users as UsersIcon,
  AtSign,
  Phone,
  Calendar,
  Search,
  Plus,
  Edit,
} from "lucide-react";

type Props = {
  searchParams: Promise<{
    q?: string;
    role?: string;
  }>;
};

export default async function AdminUsersPage({ searchParams }: Props) {
  const admin = await requirePermission("MANAGE_USERS");
  const { q = "", role = "" } = await searchParams;

  const validRole =
    role && Object.values(Role).includes(role as Role) ? (role as Role) : undefined;

  const where = {
    ...(q
      ? {
          OR: [
            { name: { contains: q, mode: "insensitive" as const } },
            { username: { contains: q, mode: "insensitive" as const } },
            { phone: { contains: q, mode: "insensitive" as const } },
          ],
        }
      : {}),
    ...(validRole ? { role: validRole } : {}),
  };

  const users = await prisma.user.findMany({
    where,
    orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
  });

  const roleLabels: Record<string, string> = {
    ADMIN: "مدیر سایت",
    SUPPORT: "پشتیبان",
    CONTENT_MANAGER: "مدیر محتوا",
    USER: "کاربر عادی",
  };

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto py-8 px-4 space-y-8 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-zinc-100">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
          <div className="p-3 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-100 rounded-2xl text-zinc-700 shadow-sm">
            <UsersIcon className="h-6 w-6" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-zinc-900 tracking-tight">مدیریت کاربران</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">
              مشاهده مشخصات کاربران، جستجوی نام/نام کاربری/موبایل و مدیریت نقش‌ها ({users.length.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} کاربر)
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button asChild size="sm" className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2">
          <Link href="/admin/users/new">
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>کاربر جدید</span>
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>

      <form
        action="/admin/users"
        className="bg-white border border-zinc-100 p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center gap-3"
      >
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search className="absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-zinc-400 h-4 w-4" />
          <input
            type="text"
            name="q"
            defaultValue={q}
            placeholder="جستجوی نام، نام کاربری یا شماره موبایل..."
            className="w-full border rounded-xl pr-10 pl-4 py-2.5 bg-zinc-50/40 text-xs focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none transition"
          />
        </div>

        <select
          name="role"
          defaultValue={validRole || ""}
          className="w-full md:w-44 border rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 bg-white text-xs focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 outline-none transition cursor-pointer"
        >
          <option value="">همه نقش‌ها</option>
          <option value="USER">کاربر عادی</option>
          <option value="SUPPORT">پشتیبان</option>
          <option value="CONTENT_MANAGER">مدیر محتوا</option>
          <option value="ADMIN">مدیر سایت</option>
        </select>

        <Button
          type="submit"
          size="sm"
          className="w-full md:w-auto h-10 rounded-xl px-5 bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-semibold"
        >
          <span>جستجو و فیلتر</span>
        </Button>
      </form>

      {users.length === 0 ? (
        <Card className="border-dashed border-zinc-200 p-12 text-center bg-zinc-50/20">
          <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-4">
            <div className="p-4 bg-zinc-50 rounded-full text-zinc-400 border border-zinc-100">
              <UsersIcon className="h-10 w-10" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-800">کاربری یافت نشد</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">جستجوی شما نتیجه‌ای در بر نداشت.</p>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {users.map((item) => {
            const isSelf = admin?.id === item.id;
            const userInitials = item.name ? item.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() : "U";
            const isUserAdmin = item.role === "ADMIN";
            const joinedDate = new Date(item.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("fa-IR");

            return (
              <Card
                key={item.id}
                className="border-zinc-100 shadow-sm rounded-2xl p-5 bg-white hover:shadow-md transition duration-200"
              >
                <CardContent className="p-0 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-1">
                    <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-full bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 flex items-center justify-center text-lg font-bold shadow-inner flex-shrink-0 select-none">
                      {userInitials}
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-1.5 truncate">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                        <span className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-800">{item.name}</span>
                        {isSelf && (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[9px] font-bold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-100 shadow-sm">
                            حساب شما
                          </span>
                        )}
                        <span
                          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[9px] font-bold ${
                            isUserAdmin
                              ? "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-100"
                              : "bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-600 border border-zinc-200"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {roleLabels[item.role] || item.role}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-xs text-zinc-400">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <AtSign className="h-3.5 w-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />
                          <span className="truncate font-mono" dir="ltr">{item.username}</span>
                        </span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Phone className="h-3.5 w-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />
                          <span dir="ltr">{item.phone}</span>
                        </span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />
                          <span>عضویت: {joinedDate}</span>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-t md:border-t-0 pt-3 md:pt-0 border-zinc-50 flex-shrink-0">
                    <Button
                      asChild
                      variant="outline"
                      size="sm"
                      className="rounded-xl h-9 text-xs font-semibold border-zinc-200 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-50"
                    >
                      <Link href={`/admin/users/${item.id}/edit`}>
                        <Edit className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-500" />
                        <span>ویرایش</span>
                      </Link>
                    </Button>

                    <UserRowActions
                      userId={item.id}
                      userName={item.name}
                      isUserAdmin={isUserAdmin}
                      isSelf={isSelf}
                    />
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `app/admin/page.tsx`
```tsx
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requirePermission } from "@/lib/rbac-server";
import Link from "next/link";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { OrderBadge } from "@/components/admin/order-badge";

import {
  Users,
  Package,
  ShoppingCart,
  TrendingUp,
  Clock,
  ArrowUpRight,
  LayoutDashboard,
} from "lucide-react";

export default async function AdminPage() {
  await requirePermission("VIEW_ADMIN_DASHBOARD");

  const [usersCount, productsCount, ordersCount, salesAgg, latestOrders] =
    await Promise.all([
      prisma.user.count(),
      prisma.product.count(),
      prisma.order.count(),
      prisma.order.aggregate({
        where: {
          status: { in: ["PAID", "PROCESSING", "SHIPPED", "DELIVERED"] },
        },
        _sum: { totalPrice: true },
      }),
      prisma.order.findMany({
        orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
        take: 5,
        include: { user: true },
      }),
    ]);

  const totalSales = Number(salesAgg._sum.totalPrice ?? 0);

  const stats = [
    {
      title: "کل فروش",
      value: totalSales.toLocaleString("fa-IR") + " تومان",
      icon: TrendingUp,
      description: "مجموع درآمدهای درگاه بانکی",
      colorClass: "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 border-emerald-100",
    },
    {
      title: "سفارشات",
      value: ordersCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR") + " سفارش",
      icon: ShoppingCart,
      description: "کل تراکنش‌های ثبت‌شده",
      colorClass: "bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 border-blue-100",
    },
    {
      title: "محصولات",
      value: productsCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR") + " کالا",
      icon: Package,
      description: "کالاهای موجود در انبار",
      colorClass: "bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 border-amber-100",
    },
    {
      title: "کاربران",
      value: usersCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR") + " کاربر",
      icon: Users,
      description: "مشتریان ثبت‌نام کرده در سایت",
      colorClass: "bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 border-indigo-100",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 pb-6 border-b border-zinc-100">
        <div className="p-3 bg-zinc-50 border border-zinc-100 rounded-2xl text-zinc-700 shadow-sm">
          <LayoutDashboard className="h-6 w-6" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-zinc-900 tracking-tight">داشبورد مدیریت</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">خلاصه‌ای از آخرین آمار و فعالیت‌های فروشگاه شما</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        {stats.map((stat, i) => {
          const Icon = stat.icon;
          return (
            <Card key={i} className="border-zinc-100 shadow-sm rounded-2xl hover:shadow-md transition duration-200">
              <CardContent className="p-6 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-400 block">{stat.title}</span>
                  <span className="text-xl font-extrabold text-zinc-900 block tracking-tight">
                    {stat.value}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 block">{stat.description}</span>
                </div>
                <div className={`p-3 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm ${stat.colorClass}`}>
                  <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-zinc-800">
          <Clock className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-600" />
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">آخرین سفارش‌ها</h2>
        </div>

        {latestOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <Card className="border-dashed border-zinc-200 p-8 text-center bg-zinc-50/20">
            <CardContent className="py-4 text-xs text-zinc-400">
              هنوز هیچ سفارشی ثبت نشده است.
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {latestOrders.map((order) => {
              const orderDate = new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleDateString("fa-IR");

              return (
                <Card
                  key={order.id}
                  className="border-zinc-100 shadow-sm rounded-2xl overflow-hidden hover:shadow-md transition duration-200"
                >
                  <CardContent className="p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
                    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-4">
                      <div className="space-y-1">
                        <p className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-800">
                          مشتری: {order.user.name}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">
                          ثبت‌شده در تاریخ: {orderDate}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <OrderBadge status={order.status} />
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between sm:justify-end gap-6 border-t sm:border-t-0 pt-4 sm:pt-0 border-zinc-50">
                      <div className="flex flex-col text-right">
                        <span className="font-extrabold text-base text-zinc-950">
                          {Number(order.totalPrice).toLocaleString("fa-IR")}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[9px] text-zinc-400">تومان</span>
                      </div>

                      <Button variant="outline" size="sm" asChild className="rounded-xl border-zinc-200 h-9 px-4">
                        <Link href={`/admin/orders/${order.id}`} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold">
                          <span>جزئیات</span>
                          <ArrowUpRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-400" />
                        </Link>
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `app/api/addresses/route.ts`
```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { getCurrentUser } from "@/lib/current-user";

export async function GET() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser();

  if (!user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const addresses = await prisma.address.findMany({
    where: { userId: user.id },
    orderBy: { id: "desc" },
  });

  return NextResponse.json(addresses);
}
```

### `app/api/admin/orders/[id]/route.ts`
```typescript
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/rbac";
import { OrderStatus } from "@prisma/client";

export async function PATCH(
  req: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get("token")?.value;

  if (!token) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!hasPermission(payload.role, "MANAGE_ORDERS")) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Forbidden" }, { status: 403 });
    }
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const body = await req.json();
  const { id } = await params;

  if (!Object.values(OrderStatus).includes(body.status)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "وضعیت سفارش نامعتبر است" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const newStatus = body.status as OrderStatus;

  try {
    const updatedOrder = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const existingOrder = await tx.order.findUnique({
        where: { id },
        include: { items: true },
      });

      if (!existingOrder) {
        throw new Error("NOT_FOUND");
      }

      if (existingOrder.status === newStatus) {
        return existingOrder;
      }

      // اگر سفارش لغو شد -> موجودی اقلام به انبار برگردانده شود
      if (existingOrder.status !== "CANCELLED" && newStatus === "CANCELLED") {
        for (const item of existingOrder.items) {
          if (item.variantId) {
            await tx.productVariant.updateMany({
              where: { id: item.variantId },
              data: { stock: { increment: item.quantity } },
            });
          }
          await tx.product.updateMany({
            where: { id: item.productId },
            data: { stock: { increment: item.quantity } },
          });
        }
      }

      // اگر سفارش لغوشده دوباره فعال شد -> بررسی و کسر مجدد موجودی از انبار
      if (existingOrder.status === "CANCELLED" && newStatus !== "CANCELLED") {
        for (const item of existingOrder.items) {
          if (item.variantId) {
            const vRes = await tx.productVariant.updateMany({
              where: { id: item.variantId, stock: { gte: item.quantity } },
              data: { stock: { decrement: item.quantity } },
            });
            if (vRes.count === 0) {
              throw new Error("موجودی انبار برای فعال‌سازی مجدد این سفارش کافی نیست.");
            }
          }
          const pRes = await tx.product.updateMany({
            where: { id: item.productId, stock: { gte: item.quantity } },
            data: { stock: { decrement: item.quantity } },
          });
          if (pRes.count === 0) {
            throw new Error("موجودی انبار برای فعال‌سازی مجدد این سفارش کافی نیست.");
          }
        }
      }

      return await tx.order.update({
        where: { id },
        data: { status: newStatus },
      });
    });

    return NextResponse.json(updatedOrder);
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "NOT_FOUND") {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "سفارش یافت نشد" }, { status: 404 });
    }
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "خطا در تغییر وضعیت";
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 400 });
  }
}
```

### `app/api/categories/route.ts`
```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const parentCategories = await prisma.category.findMany({
      where: {
        parentId: null, // 👈 فقط دریافت دسته‌بندی‌های ریشه (مادر)
      },
      include: {
        subcategories: {
          orderBy: { name: "asc" }, // لود زیردسته‌بندی‌های متصل به ترتیب الفبا
        },
      },
      orderBy: {
        name: "asc",
      },
    });
    return NextResponse.json(parentCategories);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Database error" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

### `app/api/login/route.ts`
```typescript
import bcrypt from "bcrypt";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { createToken } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { username, password } = body;

    if (!username || !password) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "نام کاربری و رمز عبور الزامی است." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const identifier = String(username).trim();

    const user = await prisma.user.findFirst({
      where: {
        OR: [{ username: identifier }, { phone: identifier }],
      },
    });

    if (!user) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "نام کاربری یا رمز عبور اشتباه است." },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const valid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password);
    if (!valid) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "نام کاربری یا رمز عبور اشتباه است." },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const token = await createToken({
      userId: user.id,
      role: user.role,
    });

    const host = req.headers.get("host") || "";
    const isLocalNetwork =
      host.includes("192.168") ||
      host.includes("10.95") ||
      host.includes("10.85") ||
      host.includes("localhost") ||
      host.includes("127.0.0.1");

    const response = NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      role: user.role,
    });

    response.cookies.set("token", token, {
      httpOnly: true,
      secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production" && !isLocalNetwork,
      sameSite: "lax",
      path: "/",
      maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
    });

    return response;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "خطای سرور" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

### `app/api/logout/route.ts`
```typescript
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

export async function POST() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  cookieStore.delete("token");

  return NextResponse.json({
    success: true,
  });
}
```

### `app/api/orders/[id]/route.ts`
```typescript
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET(
  req: Request,
  // 👇 اینجا تایپ params باید حتما Promise باشد
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("token")?.value;

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Unauthorized" },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    
    const { id } = await params;
    
    const order = await prisma.order.findFirst({
      where: {
        id: id,
        userId: payload.userId as string,
      },
      include: {
        items: {
          include: {
            product: true,
            variant: true,
          },
        },
        address: true,
      },
    });

    if (!order) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Order not found" },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json(order);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Server Error" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

### `app/api/orders/my/route.ts`
```typescript
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("token")?.value;

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Unauthorized" },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const payload = await verifyToken(token);

    const orders = await prisma.order.findMany({
      where: {
        userId: payload.userId,
      },
      include: {
        items: {
          include: {
            product: true,
          },
        },
      },
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: "desc",
      },
    });

    return NextResponse.json(orders);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);

    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Server Error" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
```

### `app/api/orders/route.ts`
```typescript
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import { getFinalPrice } from "@/lib/product-price";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("token")?.value;

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "لطفاً ابتدا وارد حساب کاربری شوید." },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    let payload;
    try {
      payload = await verifyToken(token);
    } catch {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "نشست کاربری نامعتبر است. مجدداً وارد شوید." },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const body = await req.json();

    if (!body.addressId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "آدرس تحویل اجباری است" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (!body.shippingMethodId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "انتخاب روش ارسال اجباری است" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (!body.paymentMethodId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "انتخاب روش پرداخت اجباری است" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const items = body.items;
    if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "سبد خرید خالی است" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    for (const item of items) {
      if (
        !item?.id ||
        typeof item.quantity !== "number" ||
        !Number.isInteger(item.quantity) ||
        item.quantity <= 0
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "تعداد محصول نامعتبر است" }, { status: 400 });
      }
    }

    const shippingMethod = await prisma.shippingMethod.findFirst({
      where: { id: body.shippingMethodId, isActive: true },
    });
    if (!shippingMethod) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "روش ارسال انتخابی نامعتبر است" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const paymentMethod = await prisma.paymentMethod.findFirst({
      where: { id: body.paymentMethodId, isActive: true },
    });
    if (!paymentMethod) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "روش پرداخت انتخابی نامعتبر است" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const address = await prisma.address.findFirst({
      where: {
        id: body.addressId,
        userId: payload.userId,
      },
    });
    if (!address) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "آدرس معتبر یافت نشد" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const order = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      let subtotal = 0;
      const orderItemsToCreate = [];

      for (const item of items) {
        const product = await tx.product.findFirst({
          where: { id: item.id, isActive: true },
          include: { variants: true },
        });

        if (!product) {
          throw new Error(`محصول ${item.title || ""} یافت نشد یا غیرفعال است.`);
        }

        if (product.variants.length > 0 && !item.variantId) {
          throw new Error(`لطفاً تنوع محصول ${product.title} را انتخاب کنید.`);
        }

        let itemPrice = Number(product.price);
        let selectedVariant = null;

        if (item.variantId) {
          selectedVariant = product.variants.find((v) => v.id === item.variantId);
          if (!selectedVariant) {
            throw new Error(`تنوع انتخاب شده برای کالا ${product.title} یافت نشد.`);
          }

          itemPrice = selectedVariant.price
            ? Number(selectedVariant.price)
            : Number(product.price);

          const variantUpdate = await tx.productVariant.updateMany({
            where: { id: selectedVariant.id, stock: { gte: item.quantity } },
            data: { stock: { decrement: item.quantity } },
          });
          if (variantUpdate.count === 0) {
            throw new Error(`موجودی تنوع ${selectedVariant.name} کافی نیست.`);
          }
        }

        const productUpdate = await tx.product.updateMany({
          where: { id: product.id, stock: { gte: item.quantity } },
          data: { stock: { decrement: item.quantity } },
        });
        if (productUpdate.count === 0) {
          throw new Error(`موجودی کالا ${product.title} کافی نیست.`);
        }

        const finalProductPrice = getFinalPrice({
          price: itemPrice,
          offerPrice: product.offerPrice ? Number(product.offerPrice) : null,
          offerStartsAt: product.offerStartsAt,
          offerEndsAt: product.offerEndsAt,
        });

        subtotal += finalProductPrice * item.quantity;

        orderItemsToCreate.push({
          productId: product.id,
          variantId: selectedVariant?.id || null,
          quantity: item.quantity,
          price: finalProductPrice,
        });
      }

      const totalPrice = subtotal + Number(shippingMethod.price);
      const initialStatus = paymentMethod.code === "COD" ? "PROCESSING" : "PENDING";

      return await tx.order.create({
        data: {
          userId: payload.userId,
          totalPrice,
          addressId: address.id,

          shippingTitle: address.title,
          shippingFullName: address.fullName,
          shippingPhone: address.phone,
          shippingProvince: address.province,
          shippingCity: address.city,
          shippingAddressLine: address.addressLine,
          shippingPostalCode: address.postalCode,

          shippingMethodId: shippingMethod.id,
          shippingCost: shippingMethod.price,
          paymentMethodId: paymentMethod.id,
          status: initialStatus,

          items: {
            create: orderItemsToCreate,
          },
        },
      });
    });

    return NextResponse.json(order);
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    console.error("Order Checkout Error:", error);
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Server Error";
    return NextResponse.json({ error: message }, { status: 400 });
  }
} 
```

### `app/api/otp/send/route.ts`
```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { sendOtpSms } from "@/lib/sms";

const OTP_TTL_MS = 3 * 60 * 1000;       // اعتبار کد: ۳ دقیقه
const RESEND_COOLDOWN_MS = 60 * 1000;   // فاصله مجاز بین دو ارسال: ۶۰ ثانیه

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { phone, purpose = "LOGIN" } = await req.json();

    if (!phone || !/^09\d{9}$/.test(phone)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "شماره موبایل معتبر نیست. (مثال: 09123456789)" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (purpose !== "REGISTER") {
      const otpSetting = await prisma.systemSetting.findUnique({
        where: { key: "OTP_ENABLED" },
      });

      if (otpSetting && otpSetting.value === "false") {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: "ورود با رمز یکبار مصرف در حال حاضر توسط مدیریت غیرفعال شده است." },
          { status: 403 }
        );
      }
    } else {
      const existingUser = await prisma.user.findUnique({
        where: { phone },
        select: { id: true },
      });
      if (existingUser) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: "این شماره موبایل قبلاً ثبت‌نام شده است. لطفاً وارد شوید." },
          { status: 400 }
        );
      }
    }

    const existingOtp = await prisma.otpRequest.findUnique({
      where: { phone },
    });

    if (existingOtp) {
      const remainingMs = existingOtp.expiresAt.getTime() - Date.now();
      const cooldownThreshold = OTP_TTL_MS - RESEND_COOLDOWN_MS;

      if (remainingMs > cooldownThreshold) {
        const retryAfter = Math.ceil((remainingMs - cooldownThreshold) / 1000);
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error: `لطفاً ${retryAfter.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} ثانیه صبر کنید و دوباره درخواست دهید.`,
            retryAfter,
          },
          { status: 429 }
        );
      }
    }

    const code = Math.floor(10000 + Math.random() * 90000).toString();
    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + OTP_TTL_MS);

    await prisma.otpRequest.upsert({
      where: { phone },
      update: { code, expiresAt, attempts: 0 },
      create: { phone, code, expiresAt, attempts: 0 },
    });

    const isSent = await sendOtpSms(phone, code);
    if (!isSent) {
      await prisma.otpRequest.delete({ where: { phone } }).catch(() => {});
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "خطا در ارسال پیامک. لطفاً دقایقی دیگر تلاش کنید." },
        { status: 500 }
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, message: "کد تایید ارسال شد." });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "خطای سرور" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

### `app/api/otp/verify/route.ts`
```typescript
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { createToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import bcrypt from "bcrypt";
import crypto from "crypto";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { phone, code } = await req.json();

    if (!phone || !code) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "اطلاعات ارسالی ناقص است." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const otpRequest = await prisma.otpRequest.findUnique({
      where: { phone },
    });

    if (!otpRequest) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "کدی برای این شماره یافت نشد. لطفاً کد جدید دریافت کنید.", maxAttemptsReached: true },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (new Date() > otpRequest.expiresAt) {
      await prisma.otpRequest.delete({ where: { phone } });
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "کد تایید منقضی شده است. مجدداً کد بگیرید.", maxAttemptsReached: true },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (otpRequest.code !== code) {
      const nextAttempts = otpRequest.attempts + 1;

      if (nextAttempts >= 5) {
        await prisma.otpRequest.delete({ where: { phone } });
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error: "تعداد تلاش‌های ناموفق بیش از حد مجاز بود. لطفاً کد جدید دریافت کنید.",
            maxAttemptsReached: true,
          },
          { status: 400 }
        );
      }

      await prisma.otpRequest.update({
        where: { phone },
        data: { attempts: nextAttempts },
      });

      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error: `کد تایید وارد شده نامعتبر است. (${(5 - nextAttempts).toLocaleString("fa-IR")} تلاش باقی‌مانده)`,
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await prisma.otpRequest.delete({ where: { phone } });

    let user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
      where: { phone },
    });

    if (!user) {
      const randomPassword = await bcrypt.hash(crypto.randomUUID(), 10);
      user = await prisma.user.create({
        data: {
          name: `کاربر ${phone.slice(-4)}`,
          username: phone,
          phone,
          password: randomPassword,
          role: "USER",
        },
      });
    }

    const token = await createToken({
      userId: user.id,
      role: user.role,
    });

    const response = NextResponse.json({ success: true, role: user.role });

    const host = req.headers.get("host") || "";
    const isLocalNetwork =
      host.includes("192.168") ||
      host.includes("10.95") ||
      host.includes("10.85") ||
      host.includes("localhost") ||
      host.includes("127.0.0.1");

    response.cookies.set("token", token, {
      httpOnly: true,
      secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production" && !isLocalNetwork,
      sameSite: "lax",
      path: "/",
      maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
    });

    return response;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "خطای سرور" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

### `app/api/payment/create/route.ts`
```typescript
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { orderId } = body;

    if (!orderId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Order ID is required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("token")?.value;

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    let payload;
    try {
      payload = await verifyToken(token);
    } catch {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const order = await prisma.order.findFirst({
      where: {
        id: orderId,
        userId: payload.userId,
      },
      include: {
        paymentMethod: true,
        items: true,
      },
    });

    if (!order) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Order not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    if (order.status !== "PENDING") {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Order is not payable" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const TEN_MINUTES = 10 * 60 * 1000;
    const orderAge = Date.now() - new Date(order.createdAt).getTime();

    if (orderAge > TEN_MINUTES) {
      // لغو سفارش منقضی‌شده و بازگرداندن موجودی به انبار (به صورت Idempotent)
      await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        const cancelled = await tx.order.updateMany({
          where: { id: order.id, status: "PENDING" },
          data: { status: "CANCELLED" },
        });

        if (cancelled.count > 0) {
          for (const item of order.items) {
            if (item.variantId) {
              await tx.productVariant.update({
                where: { id: item.variantId },
                data: { stock: { increment: item.quantity } },
              });
            }
            await tx.product.update({
              where: { id: item.productId },
              data: { stock: { increment: item.quantity } },
            });
          }
        }
      });

      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "مهلت پرداخت این سفارش به پایان رسیده و سفارش لغو شد." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const activeMerchantId =
      order.paymentMethod?.merchantId || process.env.ZARINPAL_MERCHANT_ID;

    const response = await fetch(
      "https://sandbox.zarinpal.com/pg/v4/payment/request.json",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          merchant_id: activeMerchantId,
          amount: Number(order.totalPrice), // ⚠️ چک شود واحد پول درگاه تومان/ریال است
          callback_url: `${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL}/payment/callback`,
          description: `Order ${order.id}`,
        }),
      }
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      const errorText = await response.text();
      console.error("ZARINPAL ERROR:", errorText);
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Payment request failed" }, { status: 500 });
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    if (data?.data?.code !== 100) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Payment request failed", data }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await prisma.order.update({
      where: { id: order.id },
      data: { authority: data.data.authority },
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      url: `https://sandbox.zarinpal.com/pg/StartPay/${data.data.authority}`,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Server Error" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

### `app/api/payment/verify/route.ts`
```typescript
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { Authority, Status } = await req.json();

    if (!Authority || Status !== "OK") {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Payment failed" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const order = await prisma.order.findFirst({
      where: { authority: Authority },
      include: { paymentMethod: true },
    });

    if (!order) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Order not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    if (order.status === "PAID") {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, message: "Order already paid" });
    }

    if (order.status !== "PENDING") {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Order is not payable" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const activeMerchantId =
      order.paymentMethod?.merchantId || process.env.ZARINPAL_MERCHANT_ID;

    const response = await fetch(
      "https://sandbox.zarinpal.com/pg/v4/payment/verify.json",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          merchant_id: activeMerchantId,
          authority: Authority,
          amount: Number(order.totalPrice), // ⚠️ اگر مرچنت روی ریال است، باید currency: "IRT" بدهید یا * 10 کنید
        }),
      }
    );

    const data = await response.json();
    const code = data?.data?.code;

    if (code === 100 || code === 101) {
      await prisma.order.updateMany({
        where: { id: order.id, status: "PENDING" },
        data: {
          status: "PAID",
          refId: String(data.data.ref_id || ""),
          paidAt: new Date(),
        },
      });

      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Verification failed" }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Server Error" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```
